# DESCRIPTOR — ubuntu.slaves.black (All Discs, Alphabetical Package Matrix)

**Project:** Ubuntu Determinant · **Edition:** Ubuntu White Edition
**Project attention:** Max Rupplin — MEARVK LLC — 2026
**Source:** discs 1–4 combined · **Total rows: 2554**
**Directory overview:** see `README.md` (the archive manual) for disc layout, chunk/reassemble workflow, and tooling.

---

## What this is

A single alphabetical roll-up of the per-disc package matrices from
`1/packages/README.md`, `2/packages/README.md`, `3/packages/README.md`, and
`4/packages/README.md`. Every package from all four source discs appears here,
sorted by software name. The per-disc files remain the authoritative originals;
this master is a combined view and adds a **Disc** column for provenance.

## Columns

**Software · Disc · Years developed · Software house · Age of expertise ·
Relative value · Importance (0–83) · Improve · Description**

- **Importance (0–83)** — the "homomorphic ego concentration" score carried over
  verbatim from each disc's README (83 = foundational to computing, 0 = niche).
- **Years developed / Software house** — filled only where reliably known on the
  source discs; otherwise `TBD` (not fabricated).
- Rows are ordered **alphabetically by software name**, not by importance. Use the
  per-disc READMEs for importance-ranked ordering.

## Disc totals

| Disc | Packages | Theme |
|---|---:|---|
| 1 | 681 | Core system |
| 2 | 1494 | Libraries & languages |
| 3 | 160 | Runtime & desktop |
| 4 | 219 | System services |
| **All** | **2554** | Combined |
